          param
            "s"

        base_type
          "int"
        "const unsigned char *p = (const unsigned char *)(s.data ? s.data : \"\");\nint i = 0;\nwhile (i < s.len) {\n    unsigned c = p[i];\n    if (c < 0x80u) { i++; continue; }\n    int len; unsigned cp;\n    if ((c & 0xE0u) == 0xC0u) { len = 2; cp = c & 0x1Fu; }\n    else if ((c & 0xF0u) == 0xE0u) { len = 3; cp = c & 0x0Fu; }\n    else if ((c & 0xF8u) == 0xF0u) { len = 4; cp = c & 0x07u; }\n    else { return i; }\n    if (i + len > s.len) { return i; }\n    for (int k = 1; k < len; k++) {\n        unsigned cc = p[i + k];\n        if ((cc & 0xC0u) != 0x80u) { return i + k; }\n        cp = (cp << 6) | (cc & 0x3Fu);\n    }\n    if (len == 2 && cp < 0x80u) { return i; }\n    if (len == 3 && cp < 0x800u) { return i; }\n    if (len == 4 && cp < 0x10000u) { return i; }\n    if (cp > 0x10FFFFu) { return i; }\n    if (cp >= 0xD800u && cp <= 0xDFFFu) { return i; }\n    i += len;\n}\nreturn -1;"

        "char buf[64];\nif (isnan(f)) return pengu_string_new(\"NaN\");\nif (isinf(f)) return f < 0 ? pengu_string_new(\"-Infinity\") : pengu_string_new(\"Infinity\");\nif (f == 0.0) {\n    return signbit(f) ? pengu_string_new(\"-0.0\") : pengu_string_new(\"0.0\");\n}\nint p;\nfor (p = 1; p <= 17; p++) {\n    snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, \"%.*g\", p, f);\n    if (strtod(buf, NULL) == f) break;\n}\nif (p > 17) p = 17;\nsnprintf(buf, sizeof buf, \"%.*e\", p - 1, f);\nconst char *q = buf;\nchar sign = 0;\nif (*q == '-') { sign = '-'; q++; }\nchar digits[32];\nint nd = 0;\nwhile (*q && *q != 'e' && *q != 'E') {\n    if (*q >= '0' && *q <= '9' && nd < 31) digits[nd++] = *q;\n    q++;\n}\nif (*q == 'e' || *q == 'E') q++;\nint exp10 = atoi(q);\nwhile (nd > 1 && digits[nd - 1] == '0') nd--;\n\nchar out[96];\nint k = 0;\nif (sign) out[k++] = '-';\nif (exp10 >= -4 && exp10 < 16) {\n    if (exp10 >= 0) {\n        int int_digits = exp10 + 1;\n        if (nd <= int_digits) {\n            for (int i = 0; i < nd; i++) out[k++] = digits[i];\n            for (int i = nd; i < int_digits; i++) out[k++] = '0';\n            out[k++] = '.';\n            out[k++] = '0';\n        } else {\n            for (int i = 0; i < int_digits; i++) out[k++] = digits[i];\n            out[k++] = '.';\n            for (int i = int_digits; i < nd; i++) out[k++] = digits[i];\n        }\n    } else {\n        out[k++] = '0';\n        out[k++] = '.';\n        for (int i = 0; i < -exp10 - 1; i++) out[k++] = '0';\n        for (int i = 0; i < nd; i++) out[k++] = digits[i];\n    }\n} else {\n    out[k++] = digits[0];\n    if (nd > 1) {\n        out[k++] = '.';\n        for (int i = 1; i < nd; i++) out[k++] = digits[i];\n    }\n    out[k++] = 'e';\n    if (exp10 < 0) { out[k++] = '-'; exp10 = -exp10; }\n    else out[k++] = '+';\n    char ebuf[16];\n    snprintf(ebuf, sizeof ebuf, \"%02d\", exp10);\n    for (const char *e = ebuf; *e; e++) out[k++] = *e;\n}\nout[k] = '\\0';\nreturn pengu_string_new(out);"
